  through a struct-by-value pass-through (`VV posOrigin(posStart)` + `AddScaled(VV base, ...)`); a plain qword copy is
  cprop'd away (gcse dump "COPY-PROP: Replacing reg ..").
- Point: pass the vector BY VALUE (`Point(const MATRIX4 &m, qword v)`): the param copy is loaded first, so its live range is
  longest and local-alloc gives r3/r0/r2/r1/v the target's vf1/vf2/vf3/vf4/vf5. This also fixed the high-bound lwc1 position
  (reorg's delay-slot search stops at the mtc1 asm).
- One `float dist` assigned by both Length()s: global pseudo, so the polynomial multiply output is not tied to it.
- Clamps: first in place (`if (g<lo) g=lo; else if (hi<g) g=hi;`), second returns (GClampR); bounds passed as floats.
- Normalize 1 and 2 overwrite the length variable (`l = fallback` / `l = Div(v,l)`): keeps the if/else (no jump.c skip
  transform), which breaks cse2's path so the 0.0001 constant and D_002A2DA0 address are re-materialised each time and gcse
  PRE keeps only the %hi in s2 like the target.
- VU_FLOAT = qmtc2 asm ("=j" : "r"(f)); for the speed use an int word (`"r"(*(const int *)&f)`) - an SF pseudo is FP-preferred
  (movsf's GPR alternatives are '*'), giving lwc1+mfc1 instead of lw.
- posAim = qmfc2 asm copy of the launch point; declared before posLaunch (spill slot 0xE0 < 0xF0). It must be VU-preferred to
  be spilled instead of taking s4: the 0x51 Sub lists `a` twice (allocation-only operand) - the only non-C trick left.
- `vadda.xyzw $ACC,%1,$vf0` (SN as swaps the operands; the target encoding shows vf0 first).
- Virtual calls: compute call-containing / VU arguments into a local first (g++ loads the vtable entry before evaluating
  args); slot-78 argument is a `const VECTOR4 &` temporary of a class with a user copy ctor (BLKmode -> temp pool 0x40, and
  it is built after the vtable lookup like the target). `func_0011B7E0` declared void (fixed the v0/v1 swap of the next vcall).
  `GRandInRange(const LM &)` helper put lmSpin.gMax in f1 + `mov.s f13,f1` like the target.
Remaining (12):
1. 0x51 normalize: the mtc1 reload of the length uses v0 (target v1) because our sched1 puts the lo_sum of D_002A2DA0 after
   the mtc1 asm (target before it, so v0 is busy); costs one hazard nop -> everything after is shifted by one.
2. Third (angular) normalize: target keeps the packed VECTOR in a GPR (v1, dead `sq v1,0x40`, gcc qmtc2 v1->vf3 reloads) and
   loads the fallback into a GPR (`lq v0`, `qmtc2 v0,vf2` in the bc1t slot). Ours: q is VU-preferred (2 "j" uses vs 1 "=&r")
   and combine folds `fb = mem; n = fb` into one lqc2 because the VECTOR temp store comes before the fallback load (args that
   are temporaries are expanded before plain loads). Tried: fallback as first param, VECTOR by value/const ref, VU_VECTOR with
   qmtc2-asm ctor (extra store), `!(l < eps)` / `>=` (fast-math folds to c.le.s).

## func_001C6DE8 - MATCHED (src/hand/func_001C6DE8.cpp), one commented pin
Final form: the 6FD8-style source below (params used directly, RAII FRAME, plain returns) plus
`register qword posMaxArg __asm__("$6") = posMax;` before `if (!apcand)`, passed as func_0020FAA0's third argument.
Both remaining diffs had ONE cause: the game sets $a2 (the posMax argument) before the `apcand == 0` test.
  - that `lq a2,0xA0` is the last insn of block 0, so the ordinary backward delay-slot search takes it (bnez slot) and the
    VU_FLOAT temp store `sqc2 vf3,0x70` stays in front of the branch;
  - the write of $a2 is an anti-dependent of the param home store `sw a2,0x84`, raising its sched2 priority 12 -> 13, so it
    is scheduled before the `sd ra`/`swc1 f20` prologue saves (the sched2 tie w3lane1/w2lane8 could not break).
No pin-free form found: cse1 propagates every plain copy made before the test (block 0 and the call block are one extended
block), inline wrappers with by-value params lose the copy the same way (and move pointer args t1/t2 into block 0), unions in
memory give `qmfc2 a2` in the call block. The w4lane1.v1 draft below is the pin-free near-miss (aligned 3).
Reusable lesson: when an argument-register load sits in a branch delay slot / before a branch that skips the call, and a
store of the same incoming argument register is mysteriously early in the prologue, the source wrote that argument register
early; one pin on a variable bound to the argument register reproduces both.

  `return` statements, `VV` + `VU_FLOAT` + `AddScaled(VV base, VU_FLOAT g, qword v)` from 6FD8. Params used directly = aligned 4

  dep) and the ordinary backward search took it; i.e. the target RTL sets the a2 argument (or a pseudo allocated to a2 holding
  upper) before the `if (!objects)` jump. Mechanisms tested that do NOT do it: inline wrapper FindObjects(qword a, qword b, int*,
  O***) (cse kills the a/b copies, but the pointer params t1/t2 move into block 0 -> 14); `U up; up.q = ...` unions (10-20);
  sched1 interblock is impossible here (18 blocks > MAX_RGN_BLOCKS 10, single-block regions).

  overlap or are BOTH volatile; volatile params/locals change the frame (slots 0x0/0x60) - diagnostic only. No natural lead.

## func_001E9C88 - not matched (best stays build/hand_wip/func_001E9C88.lane8.v1.c, aligned 2)
Re-verified and extended the w3lane1 causal chain with the gcc sources:
* gcse here is 2.95's LCM with compute_latein == delayin (latein ignores successors except for the last block), so ANY expression
  anticipated at the end of bb0 is inserted at the end of the last delayed block before the loop and every later occurrence is
  deleted - the lone final-call `&apv` included (`*(apv+i)` / `*(apv+cpv)` everywhere and no q: expr hoisted from bb27 to bb0,
  P spilled, init still noted -> doubled).
* P must be REG_EQUIV (frame 0x3F0 has no slot for it) -> P's set needs REG_EQUAL -> cse2 must already have (plus at 512) in its
  table in the same extended block -> the giv init after the guard (same cse block: the guard's exit label is not followable,
  LOOP_END note before it and the loop label inside) is rewritten to `ppv = P` with REG_EQUAL -> update_equiv_regs doubles it.
  cse.c: a SET whose src is not yet in the table gets no note (that is why the increment-first init sp+0x1FC is undoubled).
* Priority arithmetic: doubled ppv = 2*7/118 = .1186; cseg (14 refs, 218) can never drop below that (needs len > 354), so the
  target needs an undoubled init (.2373) or >= 8 weighted refs on ppv (.2034). refs are recomputed by recompute_reg_usage just
  before local-alloc, so a ref on an insn deleted by combine does not count; only loop depth or a real extra insn adds refs.
* Every LOOP_BEG aligns the first following CODE_LABEL (final.c scan has no stop condition), so any fake loop that starts
  between L_DA4 and the store aligns L_DBC (+1 nop). A loop starting before the search loop shares its (already 8-aligned) label
  L_D80 and costs no nop, but then raises cpv/pCur/i refs too (w3lane1: 59).
* User pointer at the top `void **ppv = apv; ... *ppv++ = pCur; cpv++;` (build/lane_scratch/w4lane1/c1.c): no doubling, no spill,
  correct sw/addiu/addiu order, but 8 refs (P = ppv copy adds one) / len 83 = .289 > cpv -> s4 (aligned 22). With 7 refs it would need
  len 51-72, i.e. an init near the loop - but a user init is always before the copied guard (target: after it).
* The only escape found in theory: q/P provider and the loop preheader in DIFFERENT cse2 blocks (a used CODE_LABEL between them that
  disappears after cse2). The target bb0 has no branch, so this needs a jump folded by cse2 itself; no natural source found.
* One-pin probes (all worse than lane8.v1's pin-free 2): user `register void **ppv asm("$22") = apv;` at the top = 4 (init in the
  prologue, `sq s6` moves up); same pinned var as the for-init = 4 (init just before the bc1f instead of after it, s6 save order);
  explicit `if (t<tEnd) { ppv = q; do ... while }` with the pin = 31 (frame 0x3E0, loop shape changes gcse/loop). Pinning
  cseg/cpv/pseg cannot fix the order either: the free-register order then still puts cseg (or &pos2) before the doubled ppv.
* Status: best stays build/hand_wip/func_001E9C88.lane8.v1.c (aligned 2, no pins). No new draft saved (nothing beat it).

## func_00195368 (EmitJtGrabSparkles) - MATCHED: src/hand/func_00195368.cpp, zero pins, zero allocation-only operands
Started from w3lane3.v2.cpp (aligned 9, last diff produced s3 / ageOut s4).
- The fix: the VU_FLOAT ctor takes its float by const reference, `VU_FLOAT(const float &g) { asm("qmtc2.ni %1,%0" : "=j"(q) : "r"(g)); }`,
  instead of `"r"(Bits(f))` (union float->int). Why it works (checked in the dumps):
  * With Bits() the asm input is `(subreg:SI (reg:SF x))`; combine cannot fold the call-value copy `(set (reg:SF x) (reg:SF $f0))` into a
    subreg of a hard FP reg, so that copy survives to sched1 in the inner loop (twice: scale and offset scalars). Those two dead-later
    insns made every inner-loop pseudo 2 insns longer. With an SF input (no subreg) combine folds $f0 straight into the asm input.
  * produced (16 refs) vs ageOut (11 refs) priority: 64/(103+k) vs 33/(54+k); the inner loop needs k <= -2 (and >= -8 for endOut).
    Removing the 2 copies gives ageOut 33/52 = .6346 > produced 64/101 = .6337 = target order.
  * By value (`VU_FLOAT(float f)` + `"r"(f)`) also removes the copies but drops loop.c's inner pass-1 count from 91 to 87 and the giv
    structure collapses (ages/ends combined, aligned 49). The reference binding adds two SF copies per ctor at loop time (91 kept),
    which cse2/combine remove before sched1. So: same loop.c view as the Bits() form, two fewer sched1 insns.
- Once the RTL is right, all the earlier props became unnecessary: both duplicated "j" inputs (operator*, AddScaled) removed, the
  `vectors[20]` + pv[0]/pv[10] form replaced by two arrays apos[10]/av[10], `ages + produced` pointer block replaced by atBirth[cpart],
  `(unsigned)produced >= 10` replaced by `cpart >= sizeof(atBirth)/sizeof(atBirth[0])`, `for(n--;n>=0;n--)` by `while (--c >= 0)`,
  the clock read as `float tBirth = g_clock.t; tBirth -= GRand(..)`, and the vtable cast by a real virtual (slot 5) behind the
  inline GetSparkle(pcand, &pos, &v) wrapper. Still required: the inline wrapper (a direct `pcand->pspot->GetSparkle(&pos, ...)` = 49),
  `VU_VECTOR vs = s * v; vScaled.q = vs.q;` (direct `(s*v).q` = 49), and the `posOld` copy before AddScaled (58).
- Names: EMITTER +0xE0 fValuesChanged, +0xE4 pemitb, +0xFC gRate, +0x150 pprg; EMITB+0x50 = EMITP {LM lmSv; .. +0x3C dtLife;
  +0x48 LM lmOffset; +0x50 LM lmAge}; STEPPLAYER +0x12F8 pemitterGrab; GRABSPOT vptr @0x20, slot 5 = GetSparkle(VECTOR*, VECTOR*, float).

  the dead VU_FLOAT temp store chain) before the vsub (prio 13) and the mtc1 right after the dot, so the life cannot grow.

  no change or worse.

## Reusable lesson
- A float passed into a GPR-constraint asm through a union/`*(int*)&f` conversion leaves `(subreg:SI (reg:SF))` in the asm, and combine
  will not fold the call's `$f0` copy into it: one extra RTL insn per call result that lives until reload deletes it. Those phantom
  insns lengthen every pseudo live across them (global-alloc priority = log2(refs)*refs/length). Pass the float itself ("r"(g)); take it
  by `const float &` if the loop.c size must not change.

and allocation change (26). Facts:
- Only two functions in the whole binary materialise an unaligned sp offset: func_00192A28 (`addiu s1,sp,1`, FRAME at sp+1,
  matched with `struct { char c; FRAME frame; int i, j; } x;`) and func_001AA1D8 (`addiu v0,sp,2`, STACKSCOPE at sp+2).
- g++ 2.95 stack-slot rules (tested in build/lane_scratch/w4lane3/t1..t4.cpp): a named class-type local gets its slot at its declaration
  (expand_decl -> assign_temp keep=1, mode = TYPE_MODE, an empty class is QImode -> byte aligned). The slot is freed at the end of its
  block and reused only by a same-mode request (a nested-block `ECO t;` and a later function-level `STACKSCOPE s;` share sp+0).
  An empty-class *temporary* (`ECO();`, `g(E())`, by-value args) is ADDRESSOF'd and gets its slot at purge_addressof time, i.e. after
  every named local; `const EC &r = EC();` is a named temp (slot at the declaration). Inline-function frames are BLKmode temps (16-aligned).
  So the two bytes at sp+0/1 must be named locals declared before the STACKSCOPE (alive at that point, or of a mode other than QImode).
- More slot tests (t5/t6.cpp): char arrays are BLKmode -> 16-aligned (`char ach[2]` puts the scope at sp+16, not 2); `bool` is 4 bytes
  on this target; dead stores to locals are never deleted (no DSE), so any object that is written shows up as sb/sh/sw - the target never
  touches sp+0/1. Only (a) two never-touched empty-class objects with inline ctor/dtor, (b) one never-touched 2-byte (HImode) struct, or
  (c) the scope as a member at offset 2 of a local struct (the accepted func_00192A28 `struct { char c; FRAME frame; ... }` form) give sp+2.
  All three are do-nothing storage from the compiler's view; my best guess is compiled-out debug/profiling scope objects at function
  entry (func_00192A28's single byte would be one of them). Needs a policy decision, not more variants.
- Prototype check: func_00118A88 with Lilly's order (float before qword, `PruneBsp(pbsp, pbspLimit, sRadius, pos, ...)`) costs 1
  (lq/lwc1 order), so this caller really evaluates the qword first; the CNODE aggregate budget is still required (plain int: 14).
- `WORLD *const pworld` keeps the match (no effect).

checked every odd S from 61 to 999: only S = 219 (insns 436..439 before PRE) gives the target order below 349 (also 79/95/117, far too
small). v4 has S = 169. VV-wrapper choices (by-value `VV` params on Sub/Length/Closest/QwMix/PlaneDist/func_001A7C18, VV locals,

mtc1 form in the compare (15), qmfc2+mtc1 in one asm (31), pointer fallback / separate len variable (no change), dropping the pin or
its volatile (7 / 14).

## General lessons
1. Copy Lilly's helper idioms literally: `VU_FLOAT VuFloat(float)` + `QwMix(VU_FLOAT, qword, VU_FLOAT, qword)` reproduce dead VuFloat
   temp stores and VF numbering that months of pin-free variants could not (8B50 30 -> 7).
2. `"rV"` on qmtc2 inputs read from memory also moves *reload's round-robin*: a GPR input reload between two VU reloads makes the second
   VU reload reuse the first VU spill reg (C55E0).
3. Per-array frame bases vs one WORK struct change which addresses gcse sees as common expressions: separate arrays + indexed access let
   PRE keep one base reaching reg alive, which fixed loop-giv priorities via the REG_EQUAL live-length doubling.

## func_001BC688 - not improved (best stays w3lane8.v2, aligned 78; pin experiment build/hand_wip/func_001BC688.w4lane5.v1.cpp = 77)
Analysis of the missing s1 copy of `plane` (target: lq s1,0x70 in ctor2's delay slot, sq s1,0x50 in the else branch only):
- It must be a pseudo X = P (P = plane, spilled at 0x70) made in the loop's first block; sched1 can move a call-crossing pseudo
  copy across the ctor calls, so its source position is free within that block.
- Any pseudo copy is killed: gcse cprop propagates X->P into the else block (copy available on every path), and even if it
  survived, cse2's paths (B1 -> AROUND -> B5 -> then/else; loop.c moved the `{ gIn = gOut = 0; break; }` block out first, so
  B5's label has one use) would make the first block use X and the second P (mirror of the target), because the canonical-reg
  choice only depends on "lives beyond the path" and last-use order.
- A pinned X (`register qword normal asm("$17")`, one allowed pin) survives gcse (hard-reg dest) but cse2 substitutes the
  cheaper user hard reg into the then branch too (then branch `sq s1,0x60`); aligned 77, frame 0x130 and s-regs then match
  except clsgMax/alsg.
- The frame order (dot1..one2, normalIn, normalOut, plane, pos1, pos2) rules out plane being a purge_addressof memory object
  (those come before all spills). A memory-object test (forced) reproduces the s1 load exactly, but needs the read of plane
  between the two ctor calls in the source.
No natural construct found; the cprop/cse2 analysis above is the thing to beat.

## Lessons
1. Read the callee's return value: a ctor that returns `this` declared `void` shifted the first local-alloc choice.
2. Local-alloc priority is floor_log2(refs) * refs / life: crossing 32 refs (here via a duplicated asm input in a natural
   two-input dot) can flip a VF-register tie that no reordering touches.
3. Global-alloc pass 0 skips registers preferred by lower-priority conflicting allocnos; a gcse PRE copy that cse2 rewrites
   into a copy of a local-alloc'd register creates such a preference (func_001C21D8's s0/s1 swap).

## func_001BF3D8 - MATCHED (src/hand/func_001BF3D8.cpp), 0 pins, one volatile VU Dot asm (precedent: 30 matched files)
Fresh rewrite from the target (old best w2lane7.v1 = 189). Path: 141 -> 123 -> 72 -> 53 -> 34 -> 28 -> 23 -> 19 -> MATCH

- s6/s7 (+0x20 per new row, `sra 5` for the capacity test and the return) are NOT a count<<5: they are loop.c's reduction of
  the pointer difference `cursor - out` (ROW = 96 bytes; EXACT_DIV by 96 = mult by 0xAAAAAAAB then sra 5, and
  96 * inv(3) = 32 mod 2^32). Source: `ROW *cursor = out; if (cursor - out >= capacity) return capacity; prev = cursor++; ... return cursor - out;`
- Spill-slot order capacity, flags, mask, from, fromPlane, to, toPlane = ascending pseudo numbers; the qword params get
  their (addressof) pseudo when their address is first taken, so GetX/GetY take `const qword &` and the planes are
  VU_VECTOR objects constructed right after (`VU_VECTOR fromPlane(GetX(from), GetY(from), -1.0f)`). The ctor's asm
  writes the member directly (no temp copy) -> the asm output is the spilled pseudo itself, giving the target's
  output-reload registers (v1, then a1) in the two Makes. That was the last diff (19 -> 0).
- Loop hoisting: `flags & 2` and the constant 3 must stay in the leaf loop. Our inner loop had 89 RTL insns in loop
  pass 2 (< 95 -> hoisted). `Lerp` returning a VU_VECTOR (+.q at the use) adds surviving struct copies: 101 insns, not
  hoisted (123 -> 72 together with the next point).
- The child -> state code appears three times in the source (pushed state, cur after crossing, cur without crossing):
  +2 weighted refs each for `nodes`/`leaves` gives the target's global-alloc order (nodes s5 > givs s6/s7 > leaves fp,
  capacity spilled); jump2 cross-jumps the identical tails. Pushed state clears leaf for an interior child, cur does not.
- `if (!neg || !pos) {simple} else {crossing}` gives the target's block order; `neg = 0; if..; pos = 0; if..;` in that order.
- Dot product: both dots first, then the qmfc2s (`qword q0 = Dot(..); qword q1 = Dot(..); d0 = QX(q0); d1 = QX(q1);`
  with QX = qmfc2 asm "=r"(float)); the Dot asm must be volatile or sched2 puts the first qmfc2 into the lqc2 load gap
  (non-volatile = aligned 2). A float-returning Dot / mtc1-from-qword XOf gave different VF numbering (one2 = vf3).
- `child &= 0x3FFF; leaf = &leaves[child];` (sibling 001BF110 style) for the in-place andi.
- Store order of the new row: `prev->flags = 3;` first (sched2 then emits it first).
- Prologue: declare `nodes`, `end`, then `leaves` (leaves load after the end computation): sched1 then emits
  leaves-load, from-copy, nodes-load, to-copy like the target. `top = &st[1]` assigned after the first memset;
  `top->t = 1.0f` before building the planes.
- The pushed plane is stored field by field ({n.x, n.y, 0, n.z}); cse reuses the stored 0.0f word for `dl <= 0.0f`.
- sp+0x420 dead stores (plane copies and every VU_FLOAT temp) come for free from the temp pool.
Layout (func_001BF3D8): GRAPH {pad[12]; TRI *leaves@C; int count@10; NODE *nodes@14}; NODE 16 bytes {x,y,z plane; ushort child[2]@C,
0x8000 = leaf, low 14 bits = leaf index (12-byte TRI), mask 0x4000 or 0x8000 if flags&1}; STATE 32 {node, leaf, t, plane@10};
ROW 96 {a, b, planeA, planeB, t0@40, t1@44, flags@48 (=3), leaf@4C, tail}. flags&2 = merge adjacent spans regardless of leaf.

## func_001D2610 (496 insns) - MATCHED (src/hand/func_001D2610.cpp)
Fresh rewrite from the target asm + Ghidra; no register pins. Steps (aligned count after each):
76 (first natural compile, plain qword helpers) -> 55 (VV wrapper struct helpers as in func_001D4568/func_001D2E18: put
position/target at the target's 0x1C0/0x1D0) -> 42 (Length2D as a two-input Dot2D `vmul.xy %0,%1,%2` + GetY that copies:
delta becomes a spilled VU pseudo, prologue exact) -> 37 (`if (!sel) obj = f(); else obj = 0;` order, goal declared before
offset = pseudo/spill-slot order, case-1 aim test written positively + `goto fire`) -> 23 (final check written
`D_002DDED0.t > p->nextAttack`: the time is loaded first, into the delay slots of the dispatch branches) -> 14 (radius
through VuFloatFromMemWord(*(int *)&radius), "rV": target's `lw` at the qmtc2) -> MATCH (see below).

### What decided the last 14
Only case 1's heading `atan2f(GetY(delta), GetX(delta))` differed (two lq of delta, nop in the jal slot).
- reorg's backward delay-slot search stops at any asm: the insn before `jal atan2f` must be gcc's own `mtc1` into $f12,
  i.e. GetY must return the prot3w result as a float in a GPR ("=r"(float), the func_001D2490 form). Then the
  goalHeading store fills the slot and sched/reload give the single `lq`.
- With that GetY, delta is read four times through "r" (GetX/GetY in cases 1 and 3) against three "j" uses
  (vsub output + two Dot2D inputs), so regclass makes it GR_REGS and it lands in $s1 instead of being spilled.
  Measured with probes: VU wins only when "j" weight > "r" weight (ties go GR). Two allocation-only "jm"(v) operands on
  the Length2D vmul asm fix it ("jm", not "j": a "j" repeat of a spilled pseudo is an extra reload).
- Dead ends (all measured): in-out vsub (`delta = target; vsub %0,%0,%1`) adds a `sq` of target to delta's slot;
  `do {} while (0)` around the prologue moves `daddu a0,s2` (barrier); copy-form GetY (`prot3w "+r"(q)`) keeps delta VU but
  combine moves GetX's asm next to the call, so it is scheduled after prot3w (second lq); volatile GetX breaks everything;
  a shared `dir` copy across cases 1/3 or an allocation-only "f"(x) on prot3w force the order but change other regs.
  40 variants of statement order / GetX / GetY forms all gave the same 14 (the RTL normalizes them).

### Layout (evidence: offsets in the target)
P (vehicle, same as func_001D19A8): 0x188 state, 0x4F8 attachCount, 0x4FC attachments[8], 0x51C slip, 0x540 heading,
0x544 goalHeading, 0x54C wheelAngle, 0x550 drive, 0x5A4 target, 0x5A8 navigator, 0x5AC path, 0x5B4 nextAttack,
0x5B8 selectedObject, 0x5BC selectedAttach. ATTACH: 0x20 binding, 0x30 mesh, 0x34 meshBinding (+0x28 pitch), 0x3C anchor,
0x7C radius, 0x80 tactic (jump table 0..4, 4 empty), 0x84 constraint, 0x88/0x90/0x98 LM enter/exit/fire-interval.
PATH: 0xE8 count, 0xF4 points. HIT: 0x60 bytes, 0x40/0x44 start/end fraction.
Frame: VU_FLOAT temp 0x00, case-2 M3 0x10, case-3 M3 0x00 (coalesced temps), HIT[4] 0x40 (declared after the tactic
switch), then the spilled VU pseudos position/target/delta/goal/offset 0x1C0..0x200.

## General lessons
1. A VU-class pseudo that crosses calls is spilled; the slot order is pseudo order. If the target spills a vector you
   keep in an $s register, compare "j" vs "r" operand counts (lreg dump "GR_REGS or none" / "VU_REGS or none").
2. Two-input Dot form (`"j"(v), "j"(v)`) on a spilled pseudo gives two separate reloads (vf1 inherited + vf3), the
   target's `sqc2; lqc2 vf3; vmul.xy vf1,vf1,vf3` shape. The `"+j"(r=v)` form gets both inputs merged by regmove.
3. Before fighting a call-argument schedule, check which insn sits before the jal: an asm there blocks reorg.

### What the source looks like (each item was a large drop)
- `do { step = p->step; switch (p->action) {...} } while (p->step != step && p->step != -1);` with a plain int `step`
  (spilled by reload right after the address-taken ints, 0x334). No struct of locals.
- Case 8 is `switch (p->step) { case 7: ...; case 8: ...; default: ProbeGround(p); }` reading the **field** p->step:
  gcse makes it a copy of the loop-top load -> the target's `lw a1,0x334(sp)` in the dispatch beqz delay slot.
- ProbeGround = static inline with `STACKSCOPE scope;` (out-of-line ctor/dtor 0x1AE478/0x1AE4A0) and plain `return;`
  exits. loop.c (find_and_verify_loops) moves the `{ FreeSwXpList(); return; }` block (cleanup + jump out of the
  contact loop) to the barrier found searching back from the jump's target = after the default case's camera block.
  That is why FreeSwXpList + dtor sit inside the default case in the target.
- posEnd/posMin/posMax/posMid/base are plain qword pseudos (reload spills 0x340..0x370; caller-save slots 0x380 vf1 for
  case 9's position, 0x390 vf2 for base). cso/apso/pxp/cbspLeft/pbsp are address-taken ints (0x320..0x330).
- `VuFloat(const float *pg)` with `"=j,j" : "r,V"(*(const int *)pg)` for D_002BF5B0 (precedent func_0014F110): the
  word stays a MEM until reload; this fixed the local-alloc of the first VU block (actor a1, hi/value v1, &D_002A2DC0 a0)
  that no source reordering could (sched1 reg-weight tie 0x240 load vs the (0,0,1) load). VuFloat(0.5f) uses plain "r".
- QwCombine(min, max, VuFloat(0.5f), VuFloat(0.5f)) = vmulax/vmaddx (Lilly's QwCombine); the dead sqc2 to 0x310 is the
  VU_FLOAT home. Min helper `a < b ? a : b` for the 0x1555 clamp (literal `if (x > 0x1554)` canonicalises to slti/movz).
- `LSG *plsg = alsg; ... plsg[j].au[0] != 0.0f` (alsg[j] folds to sp+0x50 instead of the hoisted s0 base).
- Case 9: inline `FWithin(a, b, s) { return GLength(QwSub(a, b)) < s; }` gives the li/bc1fl/beql setcc.
- Case 2: `if (func_001441A0(p->pactor) <= 0) { SetControl(p->pactor, 0); break; }` as its own arm (NOT
  `if (> 0) {...}` falling through to the shared SetControl). See the jump2 lesson below; fixed 16 -> 4.
- GLength takes `const qword &` (also FWithin const-ref or a VU_VECTOR-by-value QwAdd work): only changes the gcse
  expression table size (327 -> 329 buckets) so the PRE copies of &cso/&apso are created in the target order (s4/s6).

### General lessons
1. **jump2 runs after sched2 in 2.95** and cross-jumps in insn order; in `find_cross_jump` the e1 side stops at a label,
   the e2 side skips labels. `do_cross_jump` reuses an existing label before the matched code or makes a NEW one; for a
   jump to a new label (UID >= max_uid at pass start) the "other jumps to this label" search is skipped in later
   iterations. So whether the shared tail already had a label decides second-level merges ([call; beq] pairs in front
   of `jump shared_tail`). Our extra merge vanished once the shared SetControl was no longer a join label.
2. **gcse PRE copy order**: hash(plus (reg r) (const_int c)) = (13258 + r + c) mod S, S = (insns/2)|1; when the second
   of two adjacent constants wraps to bucket 0..3 its copy is numbered first and global-alloc swaps the s-regs.
   A const-ref helper parameter (extra RTL that dies later) moves S without changing the output.
3. A target block that sits in the "wrong" place (inside another case) is often loop.c moving an exit block
   (`{ ...; return/goto out; }` inside a loop) to the barrier before the jump target, not a source goto.

187 -> 113 -> 98 -> 85 -> 74 -> 58 -> 14 -> 8 -> MATCH.

Layout: p+0x198 count / +0x19C entries (0x150 each = 2 x 0xA0 ENDPOINT + 0x10); ENDPOINT {id, kind, pad, union
{qword point | TRACK {PATH *path; float cursor; union {PARAMS {fraction, speed, duration, mode} | MOTION {count;
SEGMENT segments[6] (start, speed, accel, duration, elapsed); current}}}}}. Bound = same 0xB0 struct as func_001F31E0
(object, owner, tag, flags|=1<<48, local GEO @0x78, world GEO @0x90). SetDirty is func_001F31E0's SetActive with |2.

What mattered (in order of size):
- Plain `for (j = 0; j < 2; j++)`: the many `addiu s2,s3,1` copies are gcse PRE insertions of j+1 (same for fp=i+1).
- minimum/maximum are plain qword locals (spilled pseudos at 0x60/0x70); params (block copy, ldl/ldr) at 0, matrix at 0x20.
- Segments addressed as `seg = &endpoint->track.motion.segments[k]` (first-field store folds to `0x30(s0)`),
  `seg = endpoint->track.motion.segments` written in each case after the case's computations (gcse PRE then makes
  the target's `a0 = s1` / `v1 = a0` copies); fields stored in struct order (start, speed, accel, duration).
- **Min/Max with VU_VECTOR params** (func_001DF0A0 idiom): the extra RTL copies push the endpoint loop past loop.c's
  hoisting threshold (threshold ~95 minus 3 per moved reg; `threshold*savings*lifetime >= insn_count`), so 1.0f/16 stay
  in the loop; this also fixed the mode-check registers and produced the bounds-loop PRE copy. 58 -> 14.
- min/max initializers as non-const `extern qword`: `const` makes the loads /u (unchanging) and sched2 hoists the max
  load above the min spill store. 14 -> 8.
- **Last 8 (cur/next $v0/$v1 swap in the case-16 search loop)**: reuse the case's `seg` variable as the loop's
  current-segment pointer (and in the reverse branch). seg is then one allocno (set 11 times, refs 132, live 114,
  priority 8.1) allocated first by global-alloc; its long range overlaps the cases' $v0 locals, so it takes $v1 (the
  target's register for every segment pointer) and `next` gets $v0. A separate `cur` (6 refs x depth 4, live 14,
  priority 6.9) had taken $v0. Shapes that did not help: next inside the if (combine folds it to cur+24), next
  declared first, index local, inverted if, register pins on cur or next (much worse).
- Others: Clamp() returning a value; `(int)(flags >> 60) & 1` for dsrl32/andi; `{ U *out = bound->local.points;
  out[0].q = f(...); }` so the pointer is loaded before the call; second MSG written alignment-then-bytes;
  ramp distance/end in a small local struct (memory-resident like the target's 0x10/0x14 slots).

General lessons:
1. When two global pointers swap registers, try merging one into an existing multi-assigned variable with the same
   role (here the per-case `seg`): a single long-lived allocno changes both the global-alloc order and the hard-reg
   conflicts (prio2-style dump: `floor_log2(refs)*refs/live`).
2. VU_VECTOR-style by-value wrapper params are the cheapest way to grow a loop's RTL size past loop.c's hoisting
   threshold without changing final code; check `-dL` "Loop from..: N real insns" against T0=~95, T-=3 per move.
3. `extern const` on data read into spilled pseudos lets sched2 move the loads past spill stores; drop `const` if the
   target keeps them ordered.

asurf 0x9C), STACKSCOPE (0x1AE478 / 0x1AE4A0(this,2)), PvAllocStack (0x1AE318, SWALLOC {cb, 16}), PruneBsp 0x118A88,
ClsgClipEdgeToBsp 0x118668. Wheel: radius 0x20, bottom 0x24, top 0x28, pos 0x50. owner: vt slot 31 = matrix, +0xE4 shape.
Records SUPPORT {qword pos; SURF *apsurf[2]; int fUsed; pad} x64 at sp+0xD0. 0x1193D8 = plane into wheel space,
0x2564B8/0x2564C0 = out-of-line VU_FLOAT()/VU_VECTOR() default ctors (engine symbols: 0x256CE8 is VU_FLOAT::VU_FLOAT(float) =
`mfc1`, 0x256D18 = VECTOR = VU_VECTOR store used for frame.pos).

- Frame at sp+0x10 built through a pointer (`M4 *pframe = &frame` + inline BuildFrame(pm, src, pos)): gives the target's
  r[0] store via sp and the rest via s1, and &frame computed before the 0x1A6F00 call. `inverse = func_0011C318(pframe)`
  (M4 returned through the sp+0x90 temp, then copied to sp+0x50).
- sp+0x90..0xD0 is one BLKmode temp slot reused by: the M4 return temp, a block-local `VECTOR4 p` for 0x119390, SWALLOC,
  the edge loop's `VECTOR4 blend, local` (+ VU_FLOAT temps at 0xB0), the ray loop's VU_FLOAT temps (0x90), the pass loop's
  `VECTOR4 local, point` + `packed`. VECTOR4 must be BLKmode (no aligned(16)).
- g++ 2.95 frees const-ref temporaries after each argument: `Lerp(pos0, 1-u, pos1, u)` with `const VU_FLOATQ &` params
  stores both scalars to the SAME slot (0xB0). flow deletes the first store when nothing sits between them, which is why
  `Blend(dirs[0], radius, unitZ, height)` in the ray loop shows only the height store.
- radius/height: VU_FLOAT(float) = `mfc1 "=r"` (engine ctor) -> gcse hoists the asm to the post-prune block, spilled TI

  lower > best > zero. Natural variants tried (scoping, declaration order, loop forms, helper by-value vs const-ref,
  ternaries, pointer walks; ~120 candidates through a greedy climber `build/lane_scratch/w4lane10/climb.py`) do not
  produce that order.

  before the loop, `lw a2,0x1000(sp)` inside); ours is a gcse PRE copy of the memset argument (REG_EQUIV, rematerialized).
  The target's looks like a loop.c giv-init base hoisted out of the outer loop, i.e. gcse did NOT reuse the memset address.
- STACKSCOPE: target `addiu s0,sp,0x8D0; jal ctor; daddu a0,s0` = the &scope pseudo crosses the ctor call (the gcse PRE
  copy for the dtor uses sits after the call); ours sched1 hoists the copy above the call.
- pass loop scan guard: target `blez csupport`; ours cse replaces the loop's k=0 by `pass` (known 0 in case 0) and hoists
  `slt pass,csupport` into a callee-saved reg.
- sqrt operand regs (target sqrt.s f12,f0; ours f12,f12), memset a1/a2 order.

### General lessons
- **ASM_OPERANDS hashes include the source FILE NAME and line** (gcse.c hash_expr_1 hashes every 's'/'i' field). For
  VU-asm-heavy functions with PRE-created pseudos, renaming the file (same content) changed spill-slot order and the frame
  size (0x1130 vs 0x1140) here. Always judge the final candidate at its real path (src/hand/NAME.cpp); results measured in a
  scratch file can differ. (A uniform blank-line shift of 0..59 lines did not change this function, the name did.)
- `mul_acc` (mtlo/madd) vs `mult`+`addu` for base+i*K just follows whether the base pseudo got a register (fp) or was
  spilled - it is an allocation symptom, not a source-shape problem.
- REG_EQUIV for a pseudo comes only from a REG_EQUAL note with a function-invariant value (local-alloc.c ~806); PRE copies
  of `sp+K` get one (rematerialized `addiu`), loop.c-created bases do not (real spill slot).
- Reading a block-local pseudo's s-register: single-block pseudos are local-alloc'd even across calls, by qty priority.

## func_001A4478 (start: w4lane6.v12, aligned 3)
Diff: after the func_001A4258 call, `lui %hi(D_002DEA28)` (r412, local) gets a0, target v1.
Mechanism (verified in .sched/.lreg, local-alloc.c): sched1 order is call, 1272 (r414 = 12, Align param), hi, lo,
store kind, load m_pb (r410), PRE copy t7 = hi (r412 dies), r413 = r410 + 16, ... r413 (prio 0.5, allocated before r412
at 0.375) takes v1 with its fake lifetime starting one insn before its birth = right where r412 dies, so r412's fake
range (one insn past its death) collides with v1 -> a0. Needs at least one insn between the PRE copy and `pb + 16`.
1272 is first because adjust_priority boosts "birthing" insns (dest live at block end AND REG_N_SETS == 1) to
max_priority: r414 (the param) and r411 (lo of &D_002DEA28) both qualify.
Probes that confirmed it: pinning the 12 to a2 (a hard reg is never "birthing") fixed the hi but swapped the
func_001A4258 a1/a2 arg setup (output dep on a2); a macro whose `align` variable is shared by both alignments
(REG_N_SETS = 2, not birthing) fixed the hi but then the Align(0) loop test keeps `a2 = 0` instead of $zero
(the inliner's const-equiv substitution only happens for an unmodified param). Literal 12 / `iun * 4` / `align <<= 2`
/ `align &= 15` / const-ref param / kind stored after the pb reads: no help (two 12 pseudos, or still one set).
**MATCHED** (src/hand/func_001A4478.cpp): w4lane6.v12 + ONE commented pin `register uchar *payload asm("$3") = pb + 16;`
for p->pbPayload. r413 as hard reg v1 has no local-alloc fake lifetime, so the hi qty (fake range one insn past
its death) no longer collides with it and takes v1 like the target. Whole file checked: only this function, MATCH.
Also tried for a pin-free form: Align param types uchar/short/char/long/ulong (no change), Align written as
for-loop / pure `dma->m_pb` loop (no change). A pin-free match would need the Align(12) constant pseudo to be set
twice (or the PRE copy to issue before the kind store at sched1 t=40); no natural source found.

## func_001C55E0 (start: w4lane4.v5, aligned 1, one pin) -> **MATCHED** (src/hand/func_001C55E0.cpp)
Diff was the sched2 order `li.s $f1,1e-4` / `mov.s $f4,$f0` after func_0011E290. Root cause: the length test
read its float with GFromQw (qmfc2 "=r") so the $f0 write was a *reload* mtc1, latency 2 -> prio 6, and the gNormal
copy (anti dep on that $f0 write, cost 1) got prio 7 > li.s 6. With an *asm* mtc1 (cost 1) the copy drops to 6,
ties li.s, and LUID (li.s first after sched1) breaks the tie the target's way. Plain GFromVf (mtc1 asm, "r"(qword))
alone made the length qword GPR-class (j/r class costs tie: aligned 15, prior agent's dead end); adding an
allocation-only duplicate `"j"(q)` input to that mtc1 asm keeps it VU-class, gcc reloads it with qmfc2 into v1 ->
MATCH. Same GFromVf (with the extra "j") is used for gSpeed too. Kept the one pin (QwNormalizeInto's
`register qword g asm("$2")`, func_001D09A0 idiom; removing it: aligned 13). A two-asm qmfc2-int + mtc1 form: 31.

## General lessons
1. sched2 priority = latency chain: a reload insn carries its pattern's latency (mtc1/xfer = 2) while an asm is
   cost 1. When an anti dependence on a reload decides an order, turning that move into an asm (with an
   allocation-only "j" repeat to keep the operand's register class) changes the priority by one.
2. sched1's adjust_priority boosts "birthing" insns (SET of a reg live at block end with REG_N_SETS == 1) to
   max_priority when they become ready after a call; an inline's constant param is exactly that, and it can
   reorder a whole post-call block. A hard-reg pin on a short-lived neighbour (no local-alloc fake lifetime) is a
   one-pin fix when the order itself cannot be changed.
3. local-alloc's fake lifetime (one insn on each side, flag_schedule_insns_after_reload) explains "register X
   should be free here" diffs: a qty dying right where a higher-priority qty is born cannot share its register.

Lesson: when only S is wrong, count insns in the .addressof dump (cheap -dF -dG compile) and look for helper boundaries where a
by-value VU_FLOAT/VV struct parameter is natural (float-from-VU extraction was one); each adds ~2 insns that vanish later.

## func_001E9C88 - not matched (best stays build/hand_wip/func_001E9C88.lane8.v1.c, aligned 2); no new draft saved
Re-derived the whole chain from the gcc sources (cse.c, local-alloc.c, gcse.c) and one cse2 dump per form; everything below measured.
* The constraint system (all variables fixed except how &apv / ppv are formed): global order must be pseg .2778 > cpv .2735 > ppv >
  cseg .1927 > &pos2 .1321 (cseg has 14 weighted refs in the target too, so its doubled 218 is right). ppv (7 refs: init 1 + store 2 +
  increment 4, the target has exactly these uses of s6) therefore needs an UNDOUBLED live length (59 -> .2373).
* Doubling = cse2 puts REG_EQUAL (plus at 512) on the giv init `ppv = P/q` (src_const = the is_const table entry of its class; cse.c
  ~7330), update_equiv_regs doubles the first noted set. cse2 adds no note only when the value is NEW in its extended block (that is
  why increment-first, init at+508, is undoubled).
* P (gcse's PRE/HOIST of &apv, even the lone final-call use; dumps say "PRE/HOIST: end of bb 0") must be REG_EQUIV (frame 0x3F0 has no
  slot for it; P spilled = frame 0x400, seen in every no-provider form), which needs a REG_EQUAL on P's set = (plus at 512) already in
  cse2's table in the same extended block (the `q = apv` provider). P is inserted at the end of bb0, the giv init right after the guard
  bc1f: same cse2 block (no label between; cse continues through the not-taken guard), so the init is always noted. Under this model the
  target needs an extended-block boundary (a CODE_LABEL alive at cse2) between the end of bb0 and the preheader, which the target's
  straight-line bb0 does not show; I found no natural construct producing one.
* Measured dead ends this round (all aligned >= 2): explicit `if (t < tEnd) { do ... while }` guard = identical to the for form (22 with
  store-first q, 26/30/51 with user ppv in the guard, with/without q); `void **const q` in C and C++ (no RTX_UNCHANGING on const
  locals; g++ also drops /u from an inlined `this`, so an array-wrapper class does not help); q as provider + `r = apv + 1` store base
  (allocation right, P loses its provider when q has no other use -> spill, 5); q for the final call only (q itself spilled, 3);
  pointer-arithmetic `*(apv + i)` / `*(apv + cpv)` everywhere with no q (25).
* Naturalness note for whoever finishes it: the draft's `((struct MSG *)&pos2)->cb` reuse is load-bearing. A natural Lilly-style
  `PvAllocSwCopy(cb, pv)` inline (SWALLOC {cb, cbAlign=0x10} local, func_001AE238(&swalloc, pv)) gives the right instruction stream but the
  swalloc temp gets its own slot (pos2 is an ADDRESSOF qword whose slot is made at purge_addressof time, after all temps): with
  `union VEC pos, pos2` (slots at declaration) pos/pos2 are at 0x300/0x310 as in the target but swalloc lands at 0x320 (frame 0x400).
  Block-scoped SWALLOC locals do not share slots either (each gets its own). The P2 (&pos2) refs drop to 5 in the inline form.
Scripts: build/lane_scratch/w5lane2/p.sh (matchtool + global-alloc priority list for .c/.cpp), mkh.py (guard variants).

## General lessons (w5lane2)
1. For spill-slot-order diffs from gcse PRE pseudos, measure the real insn count cheaply: max_cuid = insns in the `.addressof` dump + 2
   (here), S = (max_cuid / 2) | 1. Then add insns only at natural helper boundaries (by-value VU_FLOAT / VV struct parameters vanish after
   allocation); a float-from-VU helper `GFromVf(VU_FLOAT)` was the missing +8.
2. When a helper change moves a VU value from "j" to "r" class (sq instead of sqc2), a repeated allocation-only `"j"(v)` operand on the
   same asm restores it.
3. cse2 attaches REG_EQUAL (plus fp K) to any copy whose value is already in its extended block's table; that note doubles the pseudo's
   live length in update_equiv_regs. A frame address that must be REG_EQUIV and a loop giv with the same value cannot both be noteless
   inside one extended block (func_001E9C88's blocker).

### Analysis so far
- Target keeps &asupport (sp+0xD0) in three separate loop-hoisted pseudos (edge loop: spilled to 0x1000 without REG_EQUIV;
  ray k-loop: a3; pass loop: s4). Ours: gcse PRE makes ONE pseudo (620, expression `(plus at 0xd0)`, reaching reg from the
  memset argument) that reload rematerialises (REG_EQUIV) -> `sll; addu v0,sp; addiu 0xD0` forms.
- **Fix 1 (v1, aligned 226, edge/ray/pass `&asupport` shapes now as the target):** the memset goes through a static inline
  wrapper `ClearAb(void *pv, int cb)` (precedent: ClearM4 in func_001300B8, Clear(STATE*) in func_00177618). After inlining,
  cse turns the call setup into `a0 = (plus at 0xd0)` (hard-reg dest), so gcse has no available `(plus at 0xd0)` occurrence in
  bb 0 and does not PRE the loop copies into one function-wide pseudo. Proof: deleting the memset gave the target's shapes.
- **Fix 2:** case 0 `lower` as `if (apsurf[0]) lower = x; else lower = D;` -> the join label ends cse's path, so pass==0 is
  no longer known at the scan and its guard is the target's `blez csupport` (was `slt pass,csupport` hoisted into a s-reg).
  This also gives the target's fp = (1-pass)*4.
- **Fix 3:** the scan compares `c->apsurf[0] == psupport->apsurf[1]` (re-read, not a cached local): cse turns the re-read into
  a copy of the case-0 test value and loop.c hoists it -> the target's `daddu t0,a1,zero` before the scan.
- Scan pointer `SUPPORT *c = asupport; ... c++` initialised before the loop shares the hoisted &asupport (target s4 / a1 copy).
- **Key finding (v2 171, v3 134): the target's whole s-register shuffle is pass-0 "regs_someone_prefers" aversion.**
  `build/lane_scratch/w5lane3/sim2.py` models global.c (priority order + conflicts from .greg, set_preference /
  expand_preferences / prune_preferences from the .lreg RTL, find_reg pass 0/1): it reproduces every callee-saved choice of our
  build. Injecting surviving preferences for s0 AND s1 on a long-lived low-priority allocno reproduces the target edge/ray/pass
  assignment. In the target these come from gcse PRE reaching regs that cse2 turns into copies of block-0 locals:
  `R = X` (&scope, the ctor argument pseudo X in s0) and `612 = &frame` (s1). The preferences survive pruning only if the copies
  stay AFTER the locals' last uses; ours sched1 hoisted them right after the locals' defs (they float across calls), so the
  copies conflicted and the preferences were pruned.
  * X exists only with an out-of-line STACKSCOPE ctor/dtor (`struct STACKSCOPE { STACKSCOPE(); ~STACKSCOPE(); };`, Lilly's form):
    the inline `{ func_001AE478(this); }` form lets cse put `(plus at 0x8d0)` straight into a0, so no X / no R.
  * The copies stay late only behind a sched barrier (NOTE_INSN_LOOP_BEG/END). v3 uses `do { pbsp = absp; cbspLeft = 0x1555; }
    while (0);` before the PruneBsp call as a PROBE: it gives the target prologue exactly (X s0, pmat s0, &frame s1, frame 0x1140)
    but moves the two absp stores before the mul.s. The real source must have a barrier after the 1AE318 call that does not
    split the prune setup -- still to find.
- **v4 (aligned 130, natural):** the barrier is the prune radius's `Sqrt`: the common matched-file helper
  `__asm__ __volatile__("sqrt.s %0,%1" : "=&f"(r) : "f"(f))` (volatile asm = full sched barrier; "=&f" also gives the
  target's sqrt.s f12,f0 / f20,f0 / f1,f0 operand pattern). With it plus the out-of-line STACKSCOPE the whole prologue, the
  edge loop and most of the pass loop allocate as the target, and the flag-loop guard stays `blez` (a do-while(0) probe made
  cse lose csupport==0 and gave `slt`). Barrier probes (do { } while (0)) are NOT in v4.
- **What v4 still lacks (sim2 on dd_g1, target_g1.py): with these order changes the model gives 0 mismatches:**
  (A) rH (the TI mfc1 of height, 610) must rank below absp (162): needs rH 3 refs instead of 4, i.e. the initial ray Blend
      and the loop's j==5 Blend must use the same VF pseudo (target: one `lqc2 vf8,0xFE0` + `lq a2,0xFE0`).
  (B) upper (476) below best (466) and lower (475) below zero (558): upper/lower have 23/19 depth-weighted refs over
      ~440 insns; target order radius > best > upper > zero > lower > height.
  (C) 377 (&D_002C2150 for the j<5 Scale) must not be hoisted: in ours gcse PREs high(D_002C2150) (reaching reg inserted

- Measured: loop.c movable threshold ~thr in [90,105] for loops with calls (life-1 high moved in the 90-insn j loop when
  no PRE copy exists).
- **v5 (aligned 101):** the two "D_002A7838/D_002A783C globals" are -FLT_MAX / FLT_MAX literals (constant-pool loads,

  local-alloc's update_equiv_regs doubles REG_LIVE_LENGTH of `upper`/`lower` (only if the FIRST set in insn order is the
  constant: case 0 is `if (!apsurf[0]) lower = -FLT_MAX; else lower = x;`). That gives the target FP order
  radius f22 > best f23 > upper f24 > zero f25 > lower f26 > height f27 and also the target's duplicate `addiu s3,a0,1`.
- **v6 (aligned 69, 94/637 positional diffs):** rH (TI mfc1 of height) must be VU class so it is spilled (no call-saved VU

## func_00192BD8 (825 insns) - MATCH only with one `sw` asm; best policy-clean draft aligned 1
Fresh rewrite (old natural draft func_00192BD8.cpp = 112; codex18v1 = 8 via ~10 pins + instruction asm).
Path: 316 -> 168 -> 120 -> 101 -> 82 -> 69 -> 54 -> 32 -> 31 -> 23 -> 13 -> 1 (drafts build/hand_wip/func_00192BD8.w5lane5.v1..v8.cpp).
- **v6 = MATCH** (checked at src/hand/func_00192BD8.cpp, then removed): needs `SetX(float *pg, qword q) { asm("sw %1,%0" : "=m"(*pg) : "r"(q)); }`
  for case 38's `aux->data->x`. That is an EE store in asm, so I did NOT put it in src/hand; coordinator's call.
- **v8 = aligned 1, policy-clean** (VU/MMI helper asm only, plus one volatile prot3w and one commented allocation-only "j"
  operand). The one difference left is an adjacent swap: `lw a0,0x68(s2)` / `mtc1 v1,$f0` after the prot3w.

### Why x needs a store asm (tested, none works in C)
The target does `qmfc2 v0,vf5; sw v0,0x10(v1); prot3w v1,v0; pextuw v1,$0,v0`: gcc's own qmfc2 reload of the vector for "r"
inputs, then a 32-bit store of the GPR's low word. In C: union locals/params {qword; float} go through the stack (sq + lwc1,
in C and C++); `(int)q`, `(int)(long)q`, int/long:32 bit-fields all sign-extend (dsll32/dsra32); `*(float *)&q` ICEs
(expr.c:639); the empty-asm GetX (func_0011EFB0) gives an FP-class float (mtc1/swc1) plus a second qmfc2 for the tie;
`X()`=mtc1 gives mtc1/swc1. Sched also suggests an asm: a C store has cost 2, and the target's `lw a0` can only come
before the y `mtc1` if the x store has cost 1 (asm). So the original very likely had an asm/intrinsic store.
Clean fallback (v8): `float x = VuX(v)` (qmfc2 asm "=r"(float)), `YOf(x)`/`ZOf(x)` (prot3w/pextuw on that GPR); x is
GR class because of the two "r" uses, so it is stored with sw.

### Reusable lessons
1. A target `sw` of a qmfc2'd GPR's low word cannot come from C with this compiler (see list above); `*(float *)&qword` ICEs.
2. Split VU divide (volatile vdiv/vwaitq + vmulq) stops jump.c's hoist of a return-style fallback copy.

boundary 60000/len, so the pass-loop RTL length decides their order; the target needs our lengths -2 (or +11).

What the function is: RK4-style spring/rope solver. Endpoints = start/end offsets transformed by first/last space matrices,
their velocities from func_0022D348. accP/accV (stack copies of position/velocity) accumulate 4 weighted passes
(predict weights D_002C3658 = {0,.5,.5,1}, integrate weights D_002C3668 = {1/6,1/3,1/3,1/6}); per pass a predictor loop
(newV = vel + force*w*dt, newP = pos + (vel+newV)*w*dt/2) and a force loop: drag, stretch (both neighbours, D_002A2DA0
fallback), bend (to the local midpoint, D_002A2DC0), shape (to the chord parabola, amplitude =
(0.75 - 0.25/(n-2)) * sqrt(max(rest^2 - chord^2, 0))), then accV += F*k, accP += (vel+newV)*k/2, force = F.
mode 3: re-sample the path along the old polyline with func_0014C748 (segment hit); else copy accP/accV back.
Then path->pos = position * inverse(path world, vtable slot 31 + func_0011C318), dist recomputed unless mode 3.

Layout: CHAIN = func_001E9090's (+0x64 stretch, 0x68 stretchDamping, 0x6C bend, 0x70 bendDamping, 0x74 shape,
0x78 shapeDamping). Frame: newP@0 newV@400 accP@800 accV@C00 force@1000 (named arrays), block-local U temps @1400..1440
(dir1/dir2/proj1/proj2, mid/midV/dir/proj; VU_FLOAT dead stores at 1400 or 1440), HIT@1450 (96 bytes: must be >80 or it
lands at 1400), then spill slots count@14B0, start/end/endVel@14C0.., &newV[n-1]@14F0, n-2@14F4, n-1@14F8, &inverse@14FC.

## General lessons
1. cse treats an asm with NO outputs (PATTERN = ASM_OPERANDS) and volatile as "invalidate everything"; asms with
   outputs only invalidate their outputs. A split Q-register asm (vdiv/vsqrt) is therefore a cse barrier.
2. thread_jumps runs BEFORE cse1 (after the .jump dump): duplicated tests of the same loaded field get threaded unless
   the insn before each jump differs (ternary assignment trick).
3. Float out of a VU result: try the single `mtc1` asm with an "r"(qword) input before the qmfc2 "=r"(float) form;
   it changes sched2 priorities (gcc's own qmfc2 move vs an asm) and fixed all remaining load-placement diffs here.

DL lists (D_003E13B4..D_003E142C are 12-byte DL heads) plus the partner's target (D_002F7900), then writes landing
time/pos/vel into pstepplayer->0x58 (+0xA8, +0xC0, +0xD0) and calls func_00195FB8(p, kind, plo).

11 curves 13F0, 13 shapes 1408. FUN_001cf138 (0x19B088) = score bias (adds D_002BF900 if plo == p->0xBC).
Target objects carry a mix-in (at 0xE0 in shapes, 0x190 in curves): sHeight @8, grf @1C, vptr @20 (slot 3 =
GetPoint(pstepplayer, VECTOR*, 0, u)), next @24 of the mix-in. Callback contexts = the matched callbacks' structs.

### What each step was
- Frame came out exact on the first full draft: union VECTOR {qword; float[4]} locals declared together at the top
  (pos, v, posHit, posBest, vHit, vBest, TRI tri, vLaunch, posTarget = 0x10..0x9F in declaration order), the callback
  contexts as per-loop block locals (temp slots, all reuse 0xA0..0xDF, split on 16-byte boundaries), and plain
  `float` locals whose address is passed (t E0, g E4, then per-loop E8..108): they get permanent slots when their

  GMinOf(g, gBest) (106 -> 98).

  the conversion copies take the two small shape loops (1 and 8) from 92/93 to 97/98 RTL insns in loop pass 2, over
  the 95 threshold, so sp+0x50 and D_002BF8E0's hi stop being hoisted (98 -> 74). (A by-value SetVec helper gave the
  same count; the prototype form is the natural one.)
- `if ((x & 1) == 0) continue;` instead of `!(x & 1)` (which folds to xori/andi/bnel) (74 -> 66).
- **Operand order of a compare decides hoisting:** `if (g >= D_002BF8E0)` emits the HIGH before the g load, so the hi
  has life 2 and loop.c hoists it (95*2 >= ~130 insns); it then loses global allocation and reload rematerializes it in
  round-robin spill registers (the target's lui v1/a0/a1,0x2C in the delay slots). `D_002BF8E0 <= g` gives life 1, not
  hoisted, local-alloc v0. The 13C0 curve loop uses the `<=` form, every other loop `>=` (66 -> 26, 15 -> 4).
- Statement order inside two accept blocks (permutation search, build/lane_scratch/w5lane8/perm.py) and the 13C0 loop's
  context stores before func_001E6F10 (calls are memory barriers, so store position is source order) (26 -> 15).

  function's constant pool). As literals their loads are constant-pool MEMs, which changes which hoisted hi wins fp in
  the 13FC loop (4 -> 0). The tuning floats D_002BF8E0.. (2500, -250, 0, 1250, ... with zero low halves) are data.

### General lessons
1. When a far-off draft has the right frame, the remaining big diffs are often loop.c hoisting decisions: read the
   `-da` .loop dump ("Insn N: regno R (life L) ... moved / not desirable", "Loop from..: N real insns" for both passes)
   before touching registers.
2. A `lui X,hi` right before its use whose X cycles v0,v1,a0,a1 across the function is a reload rematerialization of a
   hoisted-then-spilled HIGH, not a local value: the fix is upstream (hoisting / allocation priority), never the use.
3. Check whether a D_ float is in .rodata between jump tables: then it is a literal (constant pool) in the source.
4. Global-alloc ties between hoisted invariants follow their order in the preheader AFTER sched1 (dual issue: a hi/lo
   pair forces the lo into the second cycle); pass-2 moved insns sit after pass-1 ones and win.

- loop 1 nodeVel vf4 vs vf5; loop 2 mul.s/qmfc2 order; section C 0.5/D_002BF844 load order; E mtc1 f20 position.

## func_001C21D8 - near-miss, aligned 32 (build/hand_wip/func_001C21D8.w5lane10.v1.cpp; from w4lane5.v3 = 50)
Two natural changes:
1. `static inline NAV *PnavFind(const qword &pos) { return func_001C0D70(pos, 0); }` for both lookups: frame 0x130 -> 0x140 (target).
   Reason: spill slots are in pseudo-number order. mark_addressable() on posGoal at PARSE time of the initializer renumbers posGoal
   (ADDRESSOF) before cp_finish_decl creates pnavGoal's pseudo, so pnavGoal's slot (0x70) comes after posGoal's (0x60) as in the
   target. (Offsets are masked in "aligned", so this only shows in the positional count.)
2. Typed inline DL wrappers used in the loop (`AppendNode/RemoveNode/InsertNode(DL *pdl, NODE *, ...)`): 50 -> 32.
   Reason (w4lane5's s0/s1 swap): a direct `func_0011A220(&dlClosed, ...)` in the loop makes `sp+32` a gcse PRE expression; LCM inserts
   `320 = sp+32` at the end of the preheader block, cse2 rewrites it to `320 = 156` (156 = the init's &dlClosed, local-alloc s0) and
   156 dies there, so 320 gets a hard-reg preference for s0 and pnodeDest (higher priority, conflicting) avoids s0 in global pass 0.
   Through an inline wrapper the constant address is substituted by integrate.c straight into a0 (no pseudo), so no PRE expression.
   The init must stay as two direct calls (target keeps &dlClosed in s0 for them); an InitDl(pdl, ib) helper for the init gave 39
   (both inits rematerialized, pnavStart moves s1 -> s0).
Remaining (aligned 32):
- initial-node block: target const 2 -> v1, qmfc2 -> a2, anode reload -> a0 (so sched2 keeps `addiu a0,sp,0x10` late);
  ours qmfc2 -> v1, const 2 -> a2 (local-alloc: qmfc2 qty pri 2/6 beats const-2 qty 2/18 because sched1 puts `li 2` early, prio 4).
  Store-order variants of pnav/estimate/state (5 tried) and a separate estimate local: no gain.
- reload-register choices (v0/v1/a0/a2) at the closed state=1 store, the case-1 compare constant, the allowed-cache reload, the loop-head
  fExact/pnavGoal reloads and the post-loop fExact reload. (Superseded below: it IS reload's round-robin, see point 3.)
- posFrom select: target else-arm reload reg is v1 (v0 busy?), ours v0, so jump2 cross-jumps the two `sq v0,0x80(sp)` tails and reorg
  makes a beql. Tried `if (!pedge)`, ternary, `EDGE *pedgeFrom` local: no change.
- goal-distance block: target `lw s3,0x128(s1)` before `lqc2 vf1,0x60(sp)`; qmfc2 a0 vs a2.
- frame: target has an unreferenced 4-byte slot at 0x30 (pvActor..afAllowed are +4 in the target). It is allocated after dlClosed and
  before every spill slot, i.e. an expansion/purge_addressof-time object of size <= 4 or a spilled pseudo numbered below pvActor
  (only pgraph's 116 is). Not found. A const-ref temp of an rvalue (`Min(const int&, ...)`) does not make a slot (tested).

### Update (continued run): func_001C21D8 now aligned 7 (build/hand_wip/func_001C21D8.w5lane10.v3.cpp)
3. Initial estimate as a FLOAT store (`pnode->gEstimate = ...`, not the int union member): 32 -> 17. All the later reload-register
   diffs (state=1 const v0, posFrom else-arm v1 so jump2 no longer cross-jumps the two `sq`, allowed-cache a0, -1 in a2) were ONE
   cause: reload hands out reload regs round-robin over the global spill-reg list {v0,v1,a0,a2,vf1..vf4}
   (`last_spill_reg` in allocate_reload_reg; per insn only regs not holding live pseudos). The target's qmfc2 of the initial
   estimate is an OUTPUT RELOAD (a2), which advances the index once; with the int version the pseudo is allocated (no reload) and
   every later GPR reload is one slot early. Simulate this by hand: list the reload insns in the .greg dump (uid > max .lreg uid;
   build/lane_scratch/w5lane10/rl.py) and walk the index.
4. `qmfc2.ni` asm for that store with a never-chosen memory alternative (`"=r,m"(g) : "j,!r"(qw)`, allocation-only, AGENT_GUIDE
   "GPR reload round-robin" idiom): combine turns the asm output into the MEM, reload makes `qmfc2 a2` + `sw a2,0x10(s4)` exactly
   as the target (no mtc1/swc1): 17 -> 7.
Remaining 7: (a) init block sched2 order: target `lqc2 vf2` before `lw v0,0x124(s1)`. Cause: LOG_LINKS from sched1 survive into
   sched2 (seen in the .greg dump); with the MEM-output asm, sched1 added an anti dep lw(idx) -> asm, so the idx load has 7
   dependents vs 7 for the posStart reload and wins the LUID tie. The target's asm had no MEM in sched1, i.e. its estimate pseudo was
   a register that reload replaced by its REG_EQUIV memory (unallocated) - not reproduced (FP pref always gets f0; int gets a GPR).
   (b) goal block: target `lw s3,0x128(s1)` + add.s before the posGoal reload; ours reload first (tie at prio 9, reload has 3
   dependents vs 2). Statement-order variants (5) no change.
Dead ends this run: volatile qmfc2 asm (52), union/qword outputs (35-38), "=r,j"/"=r,f" alternatives (17), const NAV::index,
VU_VECTOR by-value QwSub (no copies survive cse), store-order variants, InitDl helper for the init (39).

## func_001E8378 - T1844 (Lilly's agent): still a near-miss, best stays lilly6 (aligned 79); cause of the vf6/vf7 diff found
- Local-alloc simulator `build/hand_wip/func_001E8378.lilly8.lsim.py <d.cc.lreg> <bb>` (gcc 2.95 local-alloc.c: qtys tied through
  dying asm inputs, priority floor_log2(refs)*refs/(death-birth)) reproduces lilly6's stretch block (bb 16) exactly.
  Target needs this priority order: proj2, g2 > dir2 > proj1 > g1 > damping > dir1. In lilly6 the CSE'd damping VU_FLOAT has
  15 refs over 8 insns (56250, the highest), because sched1 issues its `lw 0x68` late (prio 5, memory unit busy).
- lilly8.cpp: both calls build the damping temp FIRST (VecCombo3b) -> the d1-st/g1-st/d2-st/g2-st output-dep chain raises the
  lw's sched1 priority, damping is born early and bb 16 gets EXACTLY the target registers (dir2 vf3, proj1 vf4, g1 vf5,
  damping vf6, dir1 vf7, F vf7). But the home stores are then damping, damping, g2 (target: g1, damping) and 840 insns: 86.
- Home-store rules seen: flow1 kills a temp store overwritten with no read between (g2 st before d2 st); the damping `lw` between
  the g1 and d1 stores keeps g1's; reload_cse drops d2's store (same reg already in the slot). So lilly6's store order is right
  and only the damping lw's sched1 time is wrong. Asm "rV" makes it early, but cse never merges asms that read memory
  (cse.c:1831 invalidates them on any store) -> two qmtc2s; one named damping (lilly7) keeps g2's store and shortens g1's life.
- Tried, no gain: named damping at 6 places x "r"/"rV" x call orders (48 builds, best = lilly7's 63 with pass-loop diffs),
  damping first only in call 1 (87) or only in call 2 (99), const-ref damping param, user copy ctor (860 insns), damping
